- **Web Cache Deception and Cache Poisoning**: Cache-layer attacks where the CDN/reverse-proxy and origin disagree on what to cache or what the cache key includes. Cache deception (steal authenticated data) lures victims to authenticated URLs the cache treats as static (.css/.js/.png trailers, semicolon path parameters). Cache poisoning (serve malicious content) leverages unkeyed headers (X-Forwarded-Host/Scheme, X-Original-URL, multiple Host) and unkeyed parameters (utm_, fbclid) reflected into cached responses. Includes fat-GET poisoning, parameter cloaking via duplicate-key parsing, and CDN-specific behaviors (Cloudflare, Akamai, Cloud… ([Aldiharley/pentestchain](https://github.com/Aldiharley/pentestchain/tree/HEAD/sources/v2/web-cache-deception))
